# Cut ledger: every paragraph of v0.11.0, kept or cut

Generated from `cut-ledger.json`. Each row is one block of v0.11.0 with its permanent ID, its word count, the decision, and the reason the cutter gave. To restore a block, set its `keep` to true in `cut-ledger.json` and rerun `tools/assemble.py`. Appendix B (Works Cited) is out of scope.
